===== .deps/p2a-astra-confirmation/check_preservation.py =====
SHA256 763b4de4cebac20f058acb4665d91e350370a4bd6d312deb1cd1669a46453b15
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,subprocess,re
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-astra-confirmation'
start=json.loads((w/'start-hashes.json').read_text())
changed=[p for p,h in start.items() if not (root/p).is_file() or hashlib.sha256((root/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=h]
assert not changed,changed
correction=subprocess.check_output(['git','diff','--name-only','4f265d9..HEAD'],text=True).splitlines()
assert set(correction)=={'scripts/guard_rustc.py','scripts/check_rng_policy.py','docs/DECISIONS.md','docs/ENVIRONMENT.md','docs/KNOWN_LIMITATIONS.md','docs/PHASE_2A_REVIEW.md','docs/TEST_MATRIX.md','docs/PHASE_2A_FV_L01_CORRECTIONS_REVIEW.md','docs/PHASE_2A_FV_L01_CORRECTIONS_VALIDATION.txt'},correction
rows=[l for l in (root/'docs/TEST_MATRIX.md').read_text().splitlines() if l.startswith('| PKI-')]
baseline=subprocess.check_output(['git','show','4f265d9:docs/TEST_MATRIX.md'],text=True)
assert len(rows)==19 and rows==[l for l in baseline.splitlines() if l.startswith('| PKI-')] and all('| PLANNED |' in l for l in rows)
ffi=(root/'trusted-client/src/pqc/ffi.rs').read_text()
assert len(re.findall(r'unsafe extern',ffi))==1 and len(re.findall(r'unsafe \{',ffi))==10
for p in (root/'trusted-client/src').rglob('*.rs'):
 if p.name!='ffi.rs':assert not re.search(r'unsafe\s*\{|unsafe\s+extern',p.read_text()),p
pin=subprocess.check_output(['git','-C','.deps/liboqs-0.16.0','rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip();assert pin=='5a1a854b0dc9f2141bdc771c555ee60c37950183'
status=subprocess.check_output(['git','-C','.deps/liboqs-0.16.0','status','--porcelain','--untracked-files=all','--ignored=matching'],text=True);assert not status
headers=[]
for p in (w/'core-target').glob('**/oqsconfig.h'):
 t=p.read_text();required=['/* #undef OQS_USE_SHA3_AVX512VL */','/* #undef OQS_USE_OPENSSL */','#define OQS_USE_PTHREADS 1','#define OQS_ENABLE_KEM_ml_kem_768 1','#define OQS_ENABLE_SIG_ml_dsa_65 1']
 assert all(v in t for v in required)
 headers.append(dict(path=str(p.relative_to(root)),sha256=hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),required=required))
assert len(headers)>=4
backend_source=(root/'.deps/phase2-vendor/getrandom-0.4.3/src/backends.rs').read_text()
values=sorted(set(re.findall(r'getrandom_backend\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"',backend_source)))
lib=(root/'trusted-client/src/lib.rs').read_text();assert values==sorted(set(re.findall(r'getrandom_backend\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"',lib)))
report=dict(head=subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip(),correction_baseline='4f265d9',changed_by_correction=correction,all_start_files_unchanged=True,start_files=len(start),pki_planned=19,unsafe_extern=1,unsafe_calls=10,liboqs_commit=pin,liboqs_clean=True,getrandom_values=values,headers=headers)
(w/'preservation.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!='headers'},indent=2));print('Fresh header checks:',len(headers))


===== .deps/p2a-astra-confirmation/complete_one.py =====
SHA256 9d8aecbfcf1652b28ff60f20f2292a6d86e52e7ff89fb8ce68677dbb912f00ed
from pathlib import Path
import os,sys,subprocess,json,time,shlex
w=Path('.deps/p2a-astra-confirmation').resolve();i=sys.argv[1]
cmd={'5':['python3','scripts/validate_phase2a.py'],'8':['make','check']}[i]
env=os.environ.copy()
for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO','CADP_LIBOQS_SOURCE'):env.pop(k,None)
env.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/'core-cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/f'single-target-{i}'))
t=time.monotonic()
with (w/f'single-{i}.log').open('w') as f:
 f.write('ENV '+json.dumps({k:env[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})+'\n$ '+shlex.join(cmd)+'\n');f.flush()
 p=subprocess.run(cmd,env=env,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
 f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
r=dict(command=cmd,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),target=env['CARGO_TARGET_DIR']);results=json.loads((w/'completed-policy-results.json').read_text());results[i]=r;(w/'completed-policy-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(r,flush=True)
assert p.returncode==0

===== .deps/p2a-astra-confirmation/finish_aggregates.py =====
SHA256 b0f560b1a68e789312b74b8598b5e631f39504c1fd6877ffec9c0df0729fe184
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,time,shlex,concurrent.futures
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-astra-confirmation'
results=json.loads((w/'completed-policy-results.json').read_text())
assert results['4']['exit']==0
commands={5:['python3','scripts/validate_phase2a.py'],8:['make','check']}
def run(i,cmd):
 env=os.environ.copy()
 for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO'):env.pop(k,None)
 target=w/f'aggregate-target-{i}'
 env.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/'core-cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(target))
 t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f'aggregate-{i}.log').open('w') as f:
  f.write('ENV '+json.dumps({k:env[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})+'\n$ '+shlex.join(cmd)+'\n');f.flush()
  p=subprocess.run(cmd,cwd=root,env=env,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 return i,dict(command=cmd,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),target=str(target))
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
 for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed([pool.submit(run,i,c) for i,c in commands.items()]):
  i,r=future.result();results[str(i)]=r;(w/'completed-policy-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(i,r,flush=True)
assert len(results)==3 and all(r['exit']==0 for r in results.values())


===== .deps/p2a-astra-confirmation/finish_policy.py =====
SHA256 c50370f0f2125b8506baf8b7048f01cc65736cc10779788db2d6a7f43562cc03
from pathlib import Path
import os, subprocess, json, time, shlex
root=Path.cwd(); w=root/'.deps/p2a-astra-confirmation'
commands={4:['python3','scripts/check_rng_policy.py'],5:['python3','scripts/validate_phase2a.py'],8:['make','check']}
results={}
for i,cmd in commands.items():
 env=os.environ.copy()
 for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO'): env.pop(k,None)
 target=w/f'final-policy-target-{i}'
 env.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/'core-cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(target))
 t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f'final-policy-{i}.log').open('w') as f:
  f.write('ENV '+json.dumps({k:env[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})+'\n$ '+shlex.join(cmd)+'\n');f.flush()
  p=subprocess.run(cmd,cwd=root,env=env,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 results[i]=dict(command=cmd,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),target=str(target))
 (w/'completed-policy-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(i,results[i],flush=True)
 if p.returncode: break
assert len(results)==3 and all(r['exit']==0 for r in results.values())

===== .deps/p2a-astra-confirmation/query_probes.py =====
SHA256 43666b435067601de10ec33cd4b945f77b5cc02f41be42fa33bc169abf7645b4
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,shutil,json,shlex
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-astra-confirmation';q=w/'queries';q.mkdir()
wrapper=root/'scripts/guard_rustc.py';rustc=shutil.which('rustc');driver=shutil.which('clippy-driver');assert rustc and driver
base=os.environ.copy()
for k in ('RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS'):base.pop(k,None)
rows=[]
def run(name,args,extra,ok,nested=True,stdin='compile_error!("must not compile query input");'):
 env=dict(base,**extra)
 command=[str(wrapper),driver,rustc,*args] if nested else [str(wrapper),rustc,*args]
 if nested:env['RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER']=driver
 p=subprocess.run(command,env=env,cwd=q,text=True,input=stdin,capture_output=True)
 (q/(name+'.log')).write_text('$ '+shlex.join(command)+'\nCLIPPY_ARGS='+repr(env.get('CLIPPY_ARGS'))+'\n'+p.stdout+p.stderr+'\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 assert (p.returncode==0)==ok,(name,p.returncode,p.stdout,p.stderr)
 if not ok:assert 'Phase 2A rustc policy rejected' in p.stderr,(name,p.stderr)
 rows.append(dict(name=name,command=command,clippy_args=env.get('CLIPPY_ARGS'),exit=p.returncode,expected_success=ok))
prefix=['-','--crate-name','___','--print=file-names']
suffix=['--crate-type','bin','--crate-type','rlib','--crate-type','dylib','--crate-type','cdylib','--crate-type','staticlib','--crate-type','proc-macro','--print=sysroot','--print=split-debuginfo','--print=crate-name','--print=cfg','-Wwarnings']
approved='--cfg=getrandom_backend="linux_getrandom"'
bads=['@'+str(w/'duplicate.rsp')+'__CLIPPY_HACKERY__','--cfg=getrandom_backend="custom"__CLIPPY_HACKERY__']
for i,bad in enumerate(bads):
 for j,args in enumerate([['-V'],['--version'],['-vV']]):run(f'identity-{i}-{j}',args,{'CLIPPY_ARGS':bad},True)
 for j,cfg in enumerate([[],['--cfg','getrandom_backend="linux_getrandom"'],[approved]]):
  for k,target in enumerate([[],['--target','x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu']]):
   run(f'metadata-{i}-{j}-{k}',prefix+cfg+target+suffix,{'CLIPPY_ARGS':bad},True)
public=q/'public.rs';public.write_text('fn main() {}\n')
metadata=prefix+[approved]+suffix
cases=[
 ('generic-print',['--print=cfg']),
 ('link-args',[str(public),'--print=link-args','-o',str(q/'forbidden')]),
 ('query-output',metadata+['-o',str(q/'forbidden')]),
 ('query-emit',metadata+['--emit=link']),
 ('query-link-args',metadata+['--print=link-args']),
 ('query-unknown',metadata+['-Dwarnings']),
 ('query-missing-print',metadata[:-2]),
 ('query-source',metadata+[str(public)]),
 ('literal-response',[approved,str(public),'@'+str(w/'duplicate.rsp')]),
 ('duplicate',[approved,approved,str(public)]),
 ('custom',[approved,'--cfg=getrandom_backend="custom"',str(public)]),
]
for name,args in cases:
 for i,channel in enumerate([{},*({'CLIPPY_ARGS':b} for b in bads)]):run(f'reject-{name}-{i}',args,channel,False)
# An otherwise approved source-bearing compilation must not receive the channel exemption.
for i,bad in enumerate(bads):run(f'artifact-channel-{i}',[approved,str(public),'--emit=metadata','-o',str(q/'forbidden')],{'CLIPPY_ARGS':bad},False)
assert not (q/'forbidden').exists()
# Sensitivity: direct trusted rustc link-args DOES create an artifact without --emit.
c=[rustc,str(public),'--print=link-args','-o',str(q/'direct-control')]
p=subprocess.run(c,cwd=q,text=True,capture_output=True);assert p.returncode==0 and (q/'direct-control').is_file()
(q/'direct-control.log').write_text('$ '+shlex.join(c)+'\n'+p.stdout+p.stderr+'\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'; artifact exists=true\n')
(w/'query-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2))
print('Queries PASS:',sum(r['expected_success'] for r in rows),'exact accepted handshakes;',sum(not r['expected_success'] for r in rows),'rejections; real rustc link-args artifact control succeeded.')

===== .deps/p2a-astra-confirmation/resume_policy.py =====
SHA256 b57204f131d282551c76a7485d71c51a1c4ccc542fca4825dbad4d51aeef1545
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,time,shlex,concurrent.futures
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-astra-confirmation'
commands={4:['python3','scripts/check_rng_policy.py'],5:['python3','scripts/validate_phase2a.py'],8:['make','check']}
# The previous execution session ended before these three suites completed.
# Preserve original partial logs; do not count them as passes.
(w/'interrupted-policy.json').write_text(json.dumps({'incomplete':[4,5,8],'reason':'execution sessions no longer available after continuation; original logs lack final exit/result','completed_core_indices':sorted(json.loads((w/'core-results.json').read_text()))},indent=2))
results={}
def run(i):
 env=os.environ.copy()
 for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO'):env.pop(k,None)
 target=w/f'completed-policy-target-{i}'
 assert not target.exists()
 env.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/'core-cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(target))
 t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f'completed-policy-{i}.log').open('w') as f:
  f.write('ENV '+json.dumps({k:env[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})+'\n$ '+shlex.join(commands[i])+'\n');f.flush()
  p=subprocess.run(commands[i],cwd=root,env=env,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 return i,dict(command=commands[i],exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),target=str(target))
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as pool:
 for f in concurrent.futures.as_completed([pool.submit(run,i) for i in commands]):
  i,r=f.result();results[i]=r;(w/'completed-policy-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(i,r,flush=True)
assert len(results)==3 and all(r['exit']==0 for r in results.values())

from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,time,json,shlex,concurrent.futures

results={}
def run(i):
 e=os.environ.copy()
 for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO'):e.pop(k,None)

 t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f'core-{i:02}.log').open('w') as f:

 i,r=pair;results[i]=r;(w/'core-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(i,r,flush=True)
h=w/'core-cargo-home';h.mkdir();assert not list(h.iterdir())

with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as pool:
 for f in concurrent.futures.as_completed([pool.submit(run,i) for i in (4,5,8)]):save(f.result())
assert len(results)==10 and all(r['exit']==0 for r in results.values())

===== .deps/p2a-astra-confirmation/verify_aggregates.py =====
SHA256 16c5110c39d848aa0ecf23de46d3f74c3f74c29d382f81cb77fbb27024c0a359
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,time,shlex,concurrent.futures
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-astra-confirmation'
results=json.loads((w/'completed-policy-results.json').read_text())
assert results['4']['exit']==0
commands={5:['python3','scripts/validate_phase2a.py'],8:['make','check']}
def run(i,cmd):
 env=os.environ.copy()
 for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO'):env.pop(k,None)
 target=w/f'verified-target-{i}'
 env.update(GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS='0',CARGO_HOME=str(w/'core-cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(target))
 t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f'verified-{i}.log').open('w') as f:
  f.write('ENV '+json.dumps({k:env[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR','GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS')})+'\n$ '+shlex.join(cmd)+'\n');f.flush()
  p=subprocess.run(cmd,cwd=root,env=env,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 return i,dict(command=cmd,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),target=str(target))
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
 for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed([pool.submit(run,i,c) for i,c in commands.items()]):
  i,r=future.result();results[str(i)]=r;(w/'completed-policy-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(i,r,flush=True)
assert len(results)==3 and all(r['exit']==0 for r in results.values())
